# Third-party software

Generated from `Cargo.lock` and the checksummed Cargo crate archives by `scripts/generate-third-party.sh`; do not edit by hand. The inventory is the union of normal and build dependencies for Pika's four native macOS/Linux host targets and its Windows client target. Dev-only packages are excluded.

Pika enables bundled SQLite. The SQLite amalgamation is included in the binary and its public-domain declaration is reproduced below with the crate notices.

The binary also statically links the Rust 1.88.0 standard library. Its complete library copyright report is included as N-76cae61dd94d.
